# Results

Plugin v2.7.0-rc1 on lab v0.3.0 (tag commit b9b179a). This is the first host run on a v0.3.0 tree (#104). A host run drives every cell through the scenario list: a cell is one DHCP source (a server such as Kea or dnsmasq) and one Docker host, both virtual machines on the lab host; a shape is the way the plugin attaches a container to the source's network (bridge, ipvlan, macvlan, and the two IPAM shapes bridge-ipam and macvlan-ipam, on which Docker's own address management hands out the addresses and the plugin does not lease); a scenario is one measured step (A1 first lease, C2 source down, D4b prefix withdrawn, and so on). Each scenario on each shape writes one verdict: PASS, FAIL, BLOCKED (a step before the measurement failed, so nothing was measured) or N/A (the scenario does not apply to that shape or source).

Fifteen cells, -j 4 with a 60 s stagger, 2026-10-29 to 2026-10-30. Wall time 12 h 20 min; peak host load 18.7, at most four cells running at once. The next run (plugin v2.7.0-rc2, [its page](v0.4.0-plugin-v2.7.0-rc2.md)) started on the same host while this run's last three cells were still going.

Totals (thirteen cells in the table, 4087 rows): 967 PASS, 931 N/A, 79 FAIL, 2110 BLOCKED. Only six cells ran their scenarios: isc-dhcp, host-ubuntu2404-isc-dhcp, openwrt, pihole, udhcpd and isc-failover (2010 rows: 967 PASS, 931 N/A, 79 FAIL, 33 BLOCKED). Six cells read BLOCKED on every row, 335 each: kea, dnsmasq, host-ubuntu2404, host-ubuntu2404-dnsmasq, host-debian11-docker2010 and kea-ha, all stopped by one lab defect (#121, below). windows is 67 BLOCKED: its image is not built yet (#14). Two cells wrote no verdicts and are not in the table: kea-relay, whose relay probe failed on the same defect, and chr, whose MikroTik guest never answered the lab (#130). Each cell's retry pass is part of its result: a first-attempt FAIL whose cause was a CreateEndpoint call (Docker asking the plugin for the container's address) running into the Docker engine's client timeout is re-run once at the end of the cell; the table shows the rerun and the first attempt is kept under `evidence/<cell>/retried/`. 27 rows were re-run: 18 PASS, 8 FAIL again, 1 BLOCKED.

Read the FAIL and BLOCKED rows with four things in mind.

- Six cells aborted at their first lease reset, 2010 BLOCKED rows (#121, a lab defect, fixed in #122). The lab's netplan for the VLAN segment named the source's parent link by its device name, not by the netplan id `seg0`; after the reset the Kea and dnsmasq sources did not come back and the kea-ha pair was not hot standby (one Kea serving, its partner ready to take over) within the minute the lab allows. The same cause gives 4 FAIL rows on E3b (VLAN parent) in isc-dhcp and host-ubuntu2404-isc-dhcp, on ipvlan and macvlan. None of these is a plugin finding.
- Environment: 43 rows in the six cells that ran (40 FAIL, 3 BLOCKED). 25 rows where the lab host was saturated: the lab's ssh connection to a VM was reset, `docker run` was refused, or CreateEndpoint still ran past the engine's client timeout on the rerun. 5 rows where the Docker host was not back from a reboot scenario (A5, A5b) in time. 13 timing rows under load: a lease window or a down window missed by seconds (C1, C2, C3, A9, A14, F2a), openwrt A8 at 9 of 10 containers, and udhcpd A6, where the install of the previous plugin tag failed. Peak load was 18.7 with four cells running at once; the run-cells load gate (#79) is below.
- Lab judges and parsers, fixed since in #133 and #136: E1/E2 after a plugin restart, 12 FAIL on isc-dhcp and host-ubuntu2404-isc-dhcp (#123: the judge expected a fresh DISCOVER, the plugin correctly resumes its lease with INIT-REBOOT, a REQUEST for the address it already holds); the ISC lease-file parser, 9 BLOCKED on host-ubuntu2404-isc-dhcp ipvlan A9 to A16 and B1 (#124: a client id with an escaped quote stopped it, "uid string ends mid-escape"); B3 on the IPAM shapes, 4 FAIL on openwrt and pihole (#126: no lease to inspect there, N/A now); isc-failover C7, 3 FAIL (#128: either failover partner may answer, the judge named one); isc-failover C5b and C5c, 4 BLOCKED (#129: the pool-split precondition); udhcpd A10 on the IPAM shapes, 2 FAIL (#131: udhcpd keys leases by MAC address and Docker gives each container a new one, so the address cannot come back, N/A now); pihole ipvlan C6b, 1 FAIL (#132: the DECLINE was seen, the judge misread the address that stayed); isc-failover bridge C2, 1 FAIL (#134: the source was down for zero seconds, so the scenario measured nothing).
- Source limits, still open: F8 (FORCERENEW, the server telling a client to renew now), 11 BLOCKED on isc-dhcp, host-ubuntu2404-isc-dhcp, isc-failover and pihole: the source handed out no option 90, the nonce the scenario needs. C10 (the source answering late), 6 BLOCKED on pihole and udhcpd (#125, in #135). openwrt C9 (the source renumbering its pool), 3 FAIL (#127, in #135).

Plugin findings:

- D4b (prefix withdrawn), 6 FAIL: isc-dhcp, host-ubuntu2404-isc-dhcp and isc-failover, on bridge and macvlan. After a router advertisement withdrew an IPv6 prefix (valid lifetime 0), the address formed from it stayed and counted down its lifetime, which is correct (plugin #1368), but the kernel's own route for the prefix stayed with it ("expires 0sec"), which is not. Plugin #1412, fixed in v2.7.0-rc2; PASS on the next page on every shape where the row ran.
- B7 (lease release on container remove), 2 FAIL: openwrt bridge, where the plugin's parent link has no address of its own and therefore sends no DHCPRELEASE, the documented behaviour of plugin #1288; and pihole ipvlan, PASS on the next page, cause not isolated.
- isc-failover ipvlan C2 (source down), 1 FAIL: the container came back with a different address. The partner that stayed up did not answer the rebind, so the lease ran out and the plugin asked for a fresh one, which is what RFC 2131 says to do. A source finding, not a plugin defect.
- D2 (SLAAC), the D3 rows (IPv6 mode auto) and F7 (pref64, the NAT64 prefix): PASS on every shape that carries them in the six cells that ran, except one F7 row in the environment group.
